# Upstream provenance and local edition

Source: https://github.com/WoYun520/gif-factory/tree/e89003a4a6ca4fd0e9e39c8859851b58c82bc368
Immutable source commit: `e89003a4a6ca4fd0e9e39c8859851b58c82bc368`. Local packaging version: `2026.11.24` (not an upstream release).

Copyright of the upstream skills, fonts, color cards and LICENSE remains with the upstream authors and font vendors. The MIT LICENSE and bundled font licenses are preserved verbatim.

## Changes and exclusions

- Vendored a slim market-local optional package: scripts, fonts, templates, references, 100 color cards, paper-mechanism visual refs, companion skills and LICENSE.
- Excluded examples/, docs/ previews, GALLERY.md, tests, and rendered preview catalogs under companion assets/ (style-catalog, english-title previews, signature media, film-title examples, ZIP packs).
- Catalog slug is gif-factory; upstream frontmatter name pixel-gif-sticker-pack is preserved in references/upstream-skill.md.
- Added bilingual market instructions and this provenance file. README.md and GUIDE.md received a short pointer that examples live at the pin.
